---

# 📘 Topic: **Multicast Delegates in C# (Delegates – Part 2)**

---

## 🔹 Quick Revision (Delegate – Part 1)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Last video me humne delegates ke baare me kya seekha tha?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* **Delegate ek type-safe function pointer hota hai**
* Delegate **method ka reference hold karta hai**
* Delegate se method call karne ke **3 steps** hote hain:

  1. **Define delegate**
  2. **Instantiate delegate**
  3. **Call delegate**
* Delegate se **static aur non-static dono methods** call ho sakte hain

---

## 🔹 Introduction to Multicast Delegates

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Multicast Delegate kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* **Normal delegate** → sirf **ek method** ka reference hold karta hai
* **Multicast delegate** → **ek se zyada methods** ka reference hold karta hai

👉 Ek hi delegate call se **multiple methods execute** ho jaate hain

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Simple words me samjhao.

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Ek delegate me **multiple methods bind** kar dete hain
* Jab delegate call hota hai → **sab methods ek-ek karke execute hote hain**

---

## 🔹 Real-Time Example: Rectangle

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Multicast delegate samajhne ke liye kaunsa example use hua hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, **Rectangle class** ka example.

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Rectangle class me kaunse methods hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, 2 methods:

1️⃣ **GetArea**

```csharp
public void GetArea(double width, double height)
{
    Console.WriteLine(width * height);
}
```

2️⃣ **GetPerimeter**

```csharp
public void GetPerimeter(double width, double height)
{
    Console.WriteLine(2 * (width + height));
}
```

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

In dono methods ki signature same hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Yes sir ✅

* Return type → **void**
* Parameters → **double width, double height**

👉 Isi wajah se hum **same delegate** use kar sakte hain

---

## 🔹 Normal Method Call (Without Delegate)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Agar delegate na use karein to kaise call karte?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

```csharp
Rectangle rect = new Rectangle();

rect.GetArea(12.34, 56.78);
rect.GetPerimeter(12.34, 56.78);
```

👉 Do alag-alag method calls karne padte hain

---

## 🔹 Step 1: Define Delegate

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Delegate kaise define karenge?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, method signature ke matching delegate:

```csharp
public delegate void RectDelegate(double width, double height);
```

✔ Return type same
✔ Parameters same

---

## 🔹 Step 2: Instantiate Delegate

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Delegate ka object kaise banega?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

```csharp
Rectangle rect = new Rectangle();
RectDelegate obj = new RectDelegate(rect.GetArea);
```

👉 Ab delegate **GetArea** method ko point kar raha hai

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Isko call kaise karenge?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

```csharp
obj.Invoke(12.34, 56.78);
```

✔ Area print ho jayega

---

## 🔹 Multicast Delegate Concept

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Ab second method ko bhi same delegate se kaise bind karein?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, **`+=` operator** use karte hain:

```csharp
obj += rect.GetPerimeter;
```

👉 Ab delegate ke paas:

* GetArea
* GetPerimeter
  dono methods ka reference hai

---

## 🔹 Step 3: Call Multicast Delegate

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Ab jab delegate call hoga to kya hoga?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

```csharp
obj.Invoke(12.34, 56.78);
```

👉 Output:

* Area print hoga
* Perimeter print hoga

✔ **Single call → multiple methods executed**

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Iska sabse bada fayda kya hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Same parameters ke saath
* Multiple methods execute karne ke liye
* Baar-baar method call likhne ki zarurat nahi

---

## 🔹 Example with Different Values

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Agar new rectangle ho to?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

```csharp
obj.Invoke(47.87, 34.8);
```

👉 Phir se:

* Area calculate
* Perimeter calculate

✔ Ek hi delegate call se

---

## 🔹 Important Rule: Method Signature Same Hona Chahiye

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Multicast delegate use karte waqt sabse important rule kya hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:
👉 **Sab methods ki signature same honi chahiye**

* Same **return type**
* Same **parameter types**

---

## 🔹 Return Value Problem in Multicast Delegates

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Agar methods **value return** karte ho to kya problem hoti hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, **sirf last method ka result milta hai** ❌

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Aisa kyun hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Delegate ek-ek karke sab methods call karta hai
* Har method ka return value **overwrite** hota jata hai
* Last method ka value hi bachta hai

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Example samjhao.

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

```csharp
public double GetArea(double w, double h)
{
    return w * h;
}

public double GetPerimeter(double w, double h)
{
    return 2 * (w + h);
}
```

Delegate call:

```csharp
double result = obj.Invoke(10, 5);
```

👉 `result` me sirf **GetPerimeter** ka value aayega

---

## 🔹 Output Parameters Issue

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Agar output parameters use karein to?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Output parameters bhi **overwrite** ho jaate hain
* Sirf last method ka output milega

---

## 🔹 Best Practice for Multicast Delegates

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Multicast delegate ke liye best practice kya hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

✔ Methods **void return type** hone chahiye
✔ **Output parameters avoid** karne chahiye
✔ Mostly **processing / notification type methods** ke liye use karein

---

## 🔹 Where Multicast Delegates Are Useful?

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Real-world use cases kya ho sakte hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Logging + Validation + Calculation ek saath
* Event handling (internally multicast delegates hi hote hain)
* Same data pe multiple operations perform karna

---

## ✅ Final Summary

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Is video ke main points summarize karo.

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* **Multicast delegate = multiple methods ka reference**
* `+=` se methods bind karte hain
* Ek delegate call → sab methods execute
* Sab methods ki **signature same** honi chahiye
* Return value methods me **sirf last result milta hai**
* Best use → **void methods**

---

📌 **Conclusion:**
👉 **Multicast Delegates powerful feature** hai jab same input pe multiple actions perform karne ho.
👉 **Events ka base concept** bhi multicast delegates hi hote hain.

---

* 🔹 **Delegate chaining diagram**
* 🔹 **Complete runnable example**

🎥 **Anonymous Methods in C# | Delegates – Part 3**

# 📘 Topic: **Anonymous Methods in C# (Delegates – Part 3)**

---

## 🔹 Quick Recap (Delegates – Part 1 & 2)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Anonymous methods samajhne se pehle, delegates ka short revision karo.

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* **Delegate = type-safe function pointer**
* Delegate **method ka reference hold karta hai**
* Delegate se method call karne ke **3 steps** hote hain:

  1. **Define delegate**
  2. **Instantiate delegate (bind method)**
  3. **Invoke delegate**
* Multicast delegates me **ek delegate se multiple methods** call ho sakte hain

---

## 🔹 Introduction to Anonymous Methods

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Anonymous method kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* **Anonymous method = method without a name**
* Delegate ke saath **directly ek code block bind** kar dete hain
* Alag se method likhne ki zarurat nahi hoti

👉 Matlab:

> **Method likho bina naam ke, directly delegate ke saath**

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Anonymous methods ka relation kiske saath hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, **sirf delegates ke saath**
Anonymous method **delegate ke bina exist nahi karta**

---

## 🔹 Normal Delegate Binding (Before Anonymous Method)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Pehle hum delegate kaise use karte the?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, pehle **named method** banate the:

```csharp
public static string Greetings(string name)
{
    return "Hello " + name + ", Good Morning";
}
```

Delegate define:

```csharp
public delegate string GreetingsDelegate(string name);
```

Delegate instantiate:

```csharp
GreetingsDelegate gd = new GreetingsDelegate(Greetings);
```

Invoke:

```csharp
string msg = gd("Scott");
Console.WriteLine(msg);
```

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Is approach me problem kya hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Chhota logic ke liye bhi **extra method likhna padta hai**
* Code **spread ho jata hai**
* Baar-baar upar-neeche scroll karna padta hai

---

## 🔹 Anonymous Method Concept

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Anonymous method me kya change hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* **Method alag se define nahi karte**
* **Delegate instantiate karte time hi logic likh dete hain**

---

## 🔹 Anonymous Method Syntax

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Anonymous method ka basic syntax kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

```csharp
delegate (parameters)
{
    // method body
}
```

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Isme kaun-kaun si cheezein nahi likhni padti?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* ❌ Method name
* ❌ Return type
* ❌ Access modifier (public / static)

✔ Sirf **delegate keyword**, **parameters**, aur **body**

---

## 🔹 Anonymous Method Example (Console App)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Greetings example anonymous method se kaise likhenge?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

```csharp
GreetingsDelegate gd = delegate (string name)
{
    return "Hello " + name + ", Good Morning";
};

string msg = gd("Scott");
Console.WriteLine(msg);
```

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Yahan return type kaise pata chala?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Delegate `GreetingsDelegate` ka return type **string** hai
* Compiler **automatically samajh leta hai**
* Isliye hume return type likhne ki zarurat nahi

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Is anonymous method ka output kya hoga?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

```text
Hello Scott, Good Morning
```

---

## 🔹 Advantages of Anonymous Methods

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Anonymous methods ke advantages kya hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

✔ **Less typing work**
✔ **Code compact ho jata hai**
✔ **Logic wahi dikhta hai jahan bind ho raha hai**
✔ Small logic ke liye **best choice**

---

## 🔹 When NOT to Use Anonymous Methods

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Anonymous methods har jagah use karna sahi hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

❌ Nahi sir

**Avoid anonymous methods when:**

* Logic **bahut bada** ho (100–200 lines)
* Code **reuse** karna ho
* Readability important ho

👉 Large logic ke liye **named method better**

---

## 🔹 Real-Time Use Case: Windows Forms (Button Click)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Anonymous methods real-time me kaha use hote hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* **Event handling**
* Specially **UI programming (WinForms, WPF)**

---

## 🔹 Dynamic Button Example (Without Anonymous Method)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Button click event normally kaise likhte hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

```csharp
b.Click += new EventHandler(B_Click);

void B_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
{
    MessageBox.Show("Hello World");
}
```

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Isme drawback kya hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Extra method likhna padta hai
* Chhota logic ke liye bhi alag function

---

## 🔹 Same Example Using Anonymous Method

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Anonymous method ke saath kaise likhenge?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

```csharp
b.Click += delegate
{
    MessageBox.Show("Hello World");
};
```

✔ No extra method
✔ Code short & clear

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

EventHandler normally kaunse parameters leta hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

```csharp
object sender, EventArgs e
```

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Anonymous method me parameters likhna compulsory hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Agar **parameters use nahi ho rahe**, to likhna optional
* Agar use ho rahe hain, to **likhna compulsory**

Example:

```csharp
b.Click += delegate (object sender, EventArgs e)
{
    MessageBox.Show(sender.ToString());
};
```

---

## 🔹 Important Technical Point

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Anonymous method delegate ke signature ko kaise follow karta hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Anonymous method ka **parameter list delegate ke signature se match** karta hai
* EventHandler delegate ka signature:

```csharp
void(object sender, EventArgs e)
```

---

## 🔹 Anonymous Method vs Normal Method

| Feature     | Normal Method | Anonymous Method |
| ----------- | ------------- | ---------------- |
| Method name | Required      | ❌ Not required   |
| Code reuse  | Easy          | ❌ Not reusable   |
| Code size   | Large         | ✔ Small          |
| Best for    | Large logic   | Small logic      |
| Readability | High          | Medium           |

---

## ✅ Final Summary

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Is video ke key takeaways kya hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* **Anonymous method = method without name**
* Sirf **delegate ke saath use hota hai**
* Small logic ke liye **best**
* Event handling me **bahut common**
* Less code, fast development
* Large logic ke liye **avoid**

---

📌 **Conclusion:**
👉 **Anonymous methods** delegate usage ko **simple & compact** bana dete hain.
👉 Ye concept **Lambda Expressions** ka base hai (next level).

* 🔹 **Anonymous Method vs Lambda Expression**
* 🔹 **Interview Questions**
* 🔹 **Complete WinForms example**
* 🔹 **Delegate Part-4 notes**

Below are **detailed Q&A–style notes** for
🎥 **Lambda Expressions in C# | Delegates – Part 4**
Language: **Hindi wali English (Hinglish)**
Style: **Teacher → Student conversation**, beginner-friendly, step-by-step
With **clear explanations + examples** ✅

---

# 📘 Topic: **Lambda Expressions in C# (Delegates – Part 4)**

---

## 🔁 Quick Recap (Delegates → Anonymous Methods)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Lambda Expressions samajhne se pehle, pichhle topics ka short recap karo.

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* **Delegate** = type-safe function pointer
* Delegate ke through method call karte hain
* **Anonymous Method** = method without name

  * Delegate ke saath directly **code block bind** kar dete hain
  * Less typing, par phir bhi `delegate` keyword likhna padta hai

---

## 🔹 Lambda Expressions – Introduction

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Lambda Expression kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* **Lambda Expression = shorthand (short form) of Anonymous Method**
* Anonymous method ko **aur bhi simple** banata hai
* **delegate keyword** ki bhi zarurat nahi hoti

👉 Matlab:

> **Lambda Expression anonymous method ka ultra-short version hai**

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Lambda Expressions kyun introduce kiye gaye?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Anonymous methods me bhi:

  * `delegate` keyword likhna padta tha
  * Parameter type likhna padta tha
* **C# 3.0** me Lambda Expressions aaye
* Goal: **aur kam typing + cleaner code**

---

## 🔹 Example Setup (Same as Previous Videos)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Example ke liye kaunsa delegate use ho raha hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, same delegate:

```csharp
public delegate string GreetingsDelegate(string name);
```

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Normal named method example kya tha?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

```csharp
public static string Greetings(string name)
{
    return "Hello " + name + ", Very Good Morning";
}
```

Delegate call:

```csharp
GreetingsDelegate gd = new GreetingsDelegate(Greetings);
string msg = gd("Scott");
Console.WriteLine(msg);
```

---

## 🔹 Step 1: Anonymous Method Version

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Named method ko anonymous method me kaise badalte?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

```csharp
GreetingsDelegate gd = delegate (string name)
{
    return "Hello " + name + ", Very Good Morning";
};
```

✔ Method name nahi
✔ Return type nahi
❌ Lekin `delegate` keyword aur parameter type abhi bhi likhna padta hai

---

## 🔹 Step 2: Lambda Expression Version

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Anonymous method ko Lambda Expression me kaise likhenge?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, **Lambda operator `=>`** use karke:

```csharp
GreetingsDelegate gd = (name) =>
{
    return "Hello " + name + ", Very Good Morning";
};
```

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Yahan kya-kya simplify ho gaya?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* ❌ `delegate` keyword hata
* ❌ Parameter type likhne ki zarurat nahi
* ✔ Sirf **parameter + lambda operator + body**

---

## 🔹 More Simplification (Single Statement Lambda)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Agar body sirf ek line ki ho to?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* `{ }` aur `return` bhi hata sakte hain

```csharp
GreetingsDelegate gd = name =>
    "Hello " + name + ", Very Good Morning";
```

👉 **Most compact form**

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Return type kaise pata chalta hai compiler ko?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Delegate `GreetingsDelegate` already batata hai:

  * Input: `string`
  * Output: `string`
* Compiler **type inference** karta hai

---

## 🔹 Important Doubts Explained

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Parameter type hum likh bhi sakte hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Yes sir 👍
Optional hai:

```csharp
GreetingsDelegate gd = (string name) =>
{
    return "Hello " + name;
};
```

✔ Valid
❌ But unnecessary (extra typing)

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Lambda Expression aur Anonymous Method me core difference kya hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* **Lambda Expression** = Anonymous Method ka **shorter syntax**
* Functionality same, syntax different

---

## 🔹 Lambda Expression in Windows Forms (Real-Time Use)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

UI programming me lambda kaise use hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, **event handling** me.

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Anonymous method se button click ka example kya tha?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

```csharp
b.Click += delegate (object sender, EventArgs e)
{
    MessageBox.Show("Hello World");
};
```

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Isi ko Lambda Expression me kaise likhenge?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

```csharp
b.Click += (sender, e) =>
{
    MessageBox.Show("Hello World");
};
```

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Agar `sender` aur `e` use nahi ho rahe ho?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

```csharp
b.Click += (sender, e) =>
    MessageBox.Show("Hello World");
```

Ya aur short:

```csharp
b.Click += (_, __) =>
    MessageBox.Show("Hello World");
```

---

## 🔹 Why Lambda Expressions Are Powerful

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Lambda Expressions ke main benefits kya hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

✔ **Minimum typing**
✔ **Better readability (short logic)**
✔ **Inline logic** (logic wahi dikhta hai jahan use ho raha hai)
✔ **Modern C# coding style**

---

## 🔹 When NOT to Use Lambda Expressions

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Lambda expressions har jagah use karna sahi hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

❌ Nahi sir

Avoid when:

* Logic **bahut bada** ho (50–100+ lines)
* Code **reuse** karna ho
* Debugging complex ho

👉 Aise cases me **named methods better**

---

## 🔹 Anonymous Method vs Lambda Expression

| Feature          | Anonymous Method | Lambda Expression |
| ---------------- | ---------------- | ----------------- |
| delegate keyword | Required         | ❌ Not required    |
| Parameter type   | Mostly required  | ❌ Optional        |
| Syntax length    | Medium           | ✔ Very short      |
| Readability      | Good             | ✔ Better          |
| Introduced in    | C# 2.0           | C# 3.0            |

---

## 🔹 Final Understanding Flow

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Concepts ka proper flow kya hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

1️⃣ **Named Method + Delegate**
2️⃣ **Anonymous Method** (method without name)
3️⃣ **Lambda Expression** (shortest form)

👉 Lambda Expression = **best & modern approach**

---

## ✅ Final Summary

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Is video ke key takeaways batao.

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* **Lambda Expression = shorthand of Anonymous Method**
* `=>` is called **lambda operator**
* Delegate signature se **types auto infer** hote hain
* Event handling me **widely used**
* Code **short, clean, modern** ban jata hai
* Large logic ke liye **avoid**

---

📌 **Conclusion:**
👉 **Lambda Expressions** C# me delegate usage ko **bahut simple aur powerful** bana dete hain.
👉 Ye LINQ aur modern C# programming ka **foundation** hai.

* 🔹 **Real-time enterprise examples**

🎥 **Func, Action and Predicate Delegates in C# | Delegates – Part 5**

# 📘 Topic: **Func, Action & Predicate Delegates in C# (Delegates – Part 5)**

---

## 🔁 Quick Recap (Delegates → Lambda)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Ab tak delegates series me kya-kya seekha?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* **Delegate** = type-safe function pointer
* **Anonymous Method** = method without name
* **Lambda Expression** = anonymous method ka **shortest form**
* Ab tak hum **custom delegates** define kar rahe the

---

## 🔹 Introduction: Predefined Generic Delegates

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Is video ka main topic kya hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* **Predefined Generic Delegates**
* .NET **Base Class Library** me already defined hote hain
* Teen main delegates:

  1. **Func**
  2. **Action**
  3. **Predicate**

👉 Inka use karke **custom delegate define karne ki zarurat nahi**

---

## 🔹 Example Methods (Problem Setup)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Kaunse methods ko delegate se call karna hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, 3 methods:

### 1️⃣ Value-returning method

```csharp
public static double AddNums1(int x, float y, double z)
{
    return x + y + z;
}
```

---

### 2️⃣ Non-value-returning (void) method

```csharp
public static void AddNums2(int x, float y, double z)
{
    Console.WriteLine(x + y + z);
}
```

---

### 3️⃣ Boolean-returning method

```csharp
public static bool CheckLength(string str)
{
    return str.Length > 5;
}
```

---

## 🔹 Old Approach (Custom Delegates – Problem)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Pehle in methods ko call karne ke liye kya karte the?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Har method ke liye **alag delegate define** karna padta tha
* Example:

  * Delegate for double return
  * Delegate for void
  * Delegate for bool

👉 **Too much boilerplate code** ❌

---

## 🔹 Solution: Predefined Delegates

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Is problem ka solution kya hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:
👉 **Func, Action, Predicate delegates use karo**

---

# 🔹 1️⃣ Func Delegate

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Func delegate kab use hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Jab method **value return** karta ho
* **Last generic type = return type**
* Baaki sab **input parameters**

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

AddNums1 method ke liye Func kaise likhenge?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

```csharp
Func<int, float, double, double> f1 = AddNums1;

double result = f1.Invoke(10, 20.5f, 30.5);
Console.WriteLine(result);
```

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Func<int, float, double, double> ka matlab kya hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* `int` → first input
* `float` → second input
* `double` → third input
* **last `double` → return type**

👉 Func max **16 input + 1 output** support karta hai

---

## 🔹 Func + Lambda Expression (Modern Way)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Named method hatake Lambda kaise use karein?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

```csharp
Func<int, float, double, double> f1 =
    (x, y, z) => x + y + z;
```

✔ No method
✔ No custom delegate
✔ Clean code

---

# 🔹 2️⃣ Action Delegate

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Action delegate kab use hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Jab method **void return** karta ho
* **Return type always void**
* Sirf input parameters hote hain

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

AddNums2 ke liye Action kaise likhenge?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

```csharp
Action<int, float, double> a1 = AddNums2;

a1.Invoke(10, 20.5f, 30.5);
```

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Action me kitne parameters allowed hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* **0 se 16 input parameters**
* Output kabhi nahi hota (void)

---

## 🔹 Action + Lambda

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Lambda ke saath Action ka example?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

```csharp
Action<int, float, double> a1 =
    (x, y, z) => Console.WriteLine(x + y + z);
```

---

# 🔹 3️⃣ Predicate Delegate

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Predicate delegate kab use hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Jab method **bool return** karta ho
* **Sirf ek input parameter**
* Return type **fixed = bool**

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

CheckLength method ke liye Predicate kaise likhenge?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

```csharp
Predicate<string> p1 = CheckLength;

bool status = p1.Invoke("HelloWorld");
Console.WriteLine(status);
```

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Predicate<string> ka matlab?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Input → `string`
* Output → **bool (fixed)**

---

## 🔹 Predicate + Lambda

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Lambda ke saath Predicate ka example?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

```csharp
Predicate<string> p1 =
    str => str.Length > 5;
```

---

## 🔹 Predicate vs Func<bool>

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Kya hum Predicate ki jagah Func use kar sakte hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Yes sir 👍

```csharp
Func<string, bool> f2 =
    str => str.Length > 5;
```

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Phir Predicate kyun use karein?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* **Readability better**
* Jab condition check ho (true/false)
* Industry convention

---

## 🔹 One-Line vs Multi-Line Lambda Rule

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Single line lambda kab use karein?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* Jab **sirf ek statement** ho

```csharp
x => x > 10
```

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Multi-line logic ho to?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* `{ }` aur `return` use karna padega

```csharp
str =>
{
    if (str.Length > 5)
        return true;
    else
        return false;
}
```

---

## 🔹 Comparison Table

| Delegate      | Return Type  | Parameters | Use Case                |
| ------------- | ------------ | ---------- | ----------------------- |
| **Func**      | Any type     | 0–16       | Value-returning methods |
| **Action**    | void         | 0–16       | Non-value-returning     |
| **Predicate** | bool (fixed) | 1 only     | Condition checking      |

---

## ✅ Best Practice (Interview Tip)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Interview point of view se best practice kya hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

✔ **Custom delegate tabhi banao jab zarurat ho**
✔ Mostly **Func / Action / Predicate use karo**
✔ Lambda ke saath code **short & readable** hota hai

---

## ✅ Final Summary

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Is video ke key takeaways batao.

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir:

* **Func** → value return methods
* **Action** → void methods
* **Predicate** → bool return, single parameter
* Custom delegates ki zarurat nahi
* Lambda ke saath code **clean & modern**
* Real-world + LINQ me heavily used

---

📌 **Conclusion:**
👉 **Func, Action, Predicate** delegates C# me **delegate handling ka best & professional way** hain.
👉 Modern C# (LINQ, async, events) ka **foundation** yehi hai.

Samjho **Func, Action aur Predicate** ko use karna actually **3 simple rules** follow karta hai.

---

# ✅ Func, Action & Predicate **USE kaise kare**

---

## 🔹 STEP 1: Pehle yeh decide karo

👉 **Method kya return karta hai?**

| Method ka Output                             | Delegate Use  |
| -------------------------------------------- | ------------- |
| **Value return** (int, double, string, etc.) | **Func**      |
| **void** (kuch return nahi)                  | **Action**    |
| **bool** (true / false)                      | **Predicate** |

---

# 🔹 1️⃣ Func Delegate – **Value Return karne ke liye**

### 🔸 Rule yaad rakho

👉 **Last generic type = return type**

---

### ✅ Example 1: Method ke saath Func

```csharp
static double Add(int a, int b)
{
    return a + b;
}

Func<int, int, double> f1 = Add;

double result = f1(10, 20);
Console.WriteLine(result); // 30
```

✔ `int, int` → inputs
✔ `double` → return type

---

### ✅ Example 2: Lambda ke saath Func (Most Used)

```csharp
Func<int, int, int> sum = (x, y) => x + y;

Console.WriteLine(sum(5, 6)); // 11
```

🧠 **Use Func jab calculation / data return ho**

---

# 🔹 2️⃣ Action Delegate – **Void Method ke liye**

### 🔸 Rule yaad rakho

👉 **Action hamesha void hota hai**

---

### ✅ Example 1: Method ke saath Action

```csharp
static void PrintSum(int a, int b)
{
    Console.WriteLine(a + b);
}

Action<int, int> a1 = PrintSum;
a1(10, 20); // 30
```

---

### ✅ Example 2: Lambda ke saath Action

```csharp
Action<string> print = msg => Console.WriteLine(msg);

print("Hello Action Delegate");
```

🧠 **Use Action jab sirf kaam karna ho (print, log, save)**

---

# 🔹 3️⃣ Predicate Delegate – **Condition Check ke liye**

### 🔸 Rule yaad rakho

👉 **Sirf 1 parameter + return bool**

---

### ✅ Example 1: Method ke saath Predicate

```csharp
static bool IsEven(int num)
{
    return num % 2 == 0;
}

Predicate<int> p1 = IsEven;

Console.WriteLine(p1(10)); // true
```

---

### ✅ Example 2: Lambda ke saath Predicate (Very Common)

```csharp
Predicate<string> checkLength = str => str.Length > 5;

Console.WriteLine(checkLength("Hello"));      // false
Console.WriteLine(checkLength("HelloWorld")); // true
```

🧠 **Use Predicate jab condition / filter lagani ho**

---

# 🔹 Predicate vs Func<bool> (Interview Point)

```csharp
Func<int, bool> f = x => x > 10;
Predicate<int> p = x => x > 10;
```

✔ Dono same kaam karte hain
✔ **Predicate zyada readable lagta hai for conditions**

---

# 🔹 REAL LIFE USE (LINQ Example) ⭐⭐⭐

```csharp
List<int> numbers = new List<int> { 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6 };

// Predicate internally use hota hai
var evenNumbers = numbers.FindAll(n => n % 2 == 0);

foreach (var n in evenNumbers)
{
    Console.WriteLine(n); // 2 4 6
}
```

---

# 🔹 One Line Rule yaad rakho 🧠

```csharp
// Single line → return likhne ki zarurat nahi
x => x > 10

// Multi line → {} + return mandatory
x =>
{
    if (x > 10)
        return true;
    else
        return false;
}
```

---

# 🧠 Short Memory Trick (Interview Friendly)

👉 **F A P Rule**

* **F – Func** → *Returns value*
* **A – Action** → *No return (void)*
* **P – Predicate** → *Returns bool*

---

## ✅ Final Advice (Industry + Interview)

✔ 90% cases me **custom delegate ki zarurat nahi**
✔ **Func / Action / Predicate + Lambda = Clean Code**
✔ LINQ, Events, Async sab jagah use hota hai

✅ **MCQs**

# 📘 Extension Methods in C# (.NET) – Q&A Notes

*(C# 3.0 Feature)*

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Extension Method kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

**Extension Method** ek aisa feature hai jisse hum **existing class ya structure** me **new method add kar sakte hain**,
✔ bina source code change kiye
✔ bina recompilation
✔ bina permissions liye

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Ye feature kab introduce hua?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

**C# 3.0** me Extension Methods introduce hue.

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Problem kya thi jiske liye Extension Method aaya?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Suppose:

* Ek class me already **10 methods** hain
* Mujhe **5 new methods add** karne hain

Problems:
1️⃣ Source code available nahi
2️⃣ Class **sealed** hai
3️⃣ Class **structure** hai (inheritance possible nahi)
4️⃣ Code already tested → dobara testing nahi chahte

👉 Inheritance bhi kaam nahi karta in cases me

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Inheritance se problem ka solution nahi hota?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Partially hota hai, lekin problems hain:

* ❌ Sealed class inherit nahi hoti
* ❌ Structure inherit nahi hota
* ❌ Original class ke object se new methods call nahi kar sakte

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Extension Method ka exact definition kya hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

> **Extension Method** ek technique hai jisse hum
> **existing class ya structure me new methods add kar sakte hain**
> **bina source code modify kiye**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Extension Method banane ke rules kya hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

### ✅ Important Rules:

1️⃣ Method **static class** me hona chahiye
2️⃣ Method **static** hona chahiye
3️⃣ **First parameter** me `this` keyword hona chahiye
4️⃣ `this` ke baad **original class / structure ka type**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Simple example dikhao

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

### Existing class (Program.cs)

```csharp
class Program
{
    public void Test1()
    {
        Console.WriteLine("Method 1");
    }

    public void Test2()
    {
        Console.WriteLine("Method 2");
    }
}
```

---

### Extension Method class

```csharp
static class MyExtensions
{
    public static void Test3(this Program p)
    {
        Console.WriteLine("Method 3");
    }
}
```

---

### Use karna

```csharp
Program p = new Program();
p.Test1();
p.Test2();
p.Test3(); // Extension Method
```

✔ Test3 **Program class me added lagta hai**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Ye method static hai phir instance se kaise call ho raha?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

* Extension method **static hota hai**
* Lekin **bind hone ke baad non-static jaisa behave karta hai**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Agar same name ka method ho to kya hoga?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Agar:

* Original class me method already exist karta hai
* Extension method ka **same name + same signature** ho

👉 **Original method ko priority milti hai**
👉 Extension method call nahi hota ❌

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Binding parameter kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

```csharp
public static void Test3(this Program p)
```

* `this Program p` = **binding parameter**
* Ye sirf binding ke liye hota hai
* Call karte waqt value pass nahi karte

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Parameters ka rule kya hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

✔ Sirf **1 binding parameter** allowed
✔ Binding parameter **first position** pe hona chahiye
✔ Baaki parameters normal hote hain

👉 Agar method me **n parameters** hain
👉 Call karte waqt **n-1 parameters** pass karte hain

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Structure pe Extension Method possible hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

✅ Haan

Example: **int (Int32)** pe factorial method

```csharp
static class IntExtensions
{
    public static long Factorial(this int x)
    {
        if (x <= 1) return 1;
        return x * (x - 1).Factorial();
    }
}
```

### Use:

```csharp
int i = 5;
long result = i.Factorial();
Console.WriteLine(result); // 120
```

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Sealed class pe bhi possible hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

✅ Haan

Example: **string** (sealed class)

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: String ko Proper Case me convert karne ka example?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

```csharp
static class StringExtensions
{
    public static string ToProper(this string oldStr)
    {
        if (oldStr.Trim().Length == 0)
            return oldStr;

        oldStr = oldStr.ToLower();
        string[] words = oldStr.Split(' ');
        string newStr = null;

        foreach (string word in words)
        {
            char[] chars = word.ToCharArray();
            chars[0] = char.ToUpper(chars[0]);
            string temp = new string(chars);

            if (newStr == null)
                newStr = temp;
            else
                newStr += " " + temp;
        }
        return newStr;
    }
}
```

### Use:

```csharp
string s = "heLLo hOw aRe yOU";
Console.WriteLine(s.ToProper());
```

✔ Output: **Hello How Are You**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Extension Method ke main benefits kya hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

✔ Source code modify nahi
✔ Sealed class pe kaam karta hai
✔ Structure pe kaam karta hai
✔ Code reuse easy
✔ Clean & readable code

---

## 🧠 Final Summary (Exam / Interview)

* **Extension Methods** = add methods to existing class/structure
* Defined in **static class**
* Method must be **static**
* First parameter me **this + type**
* Original method > Extension method (priority)

---

* 🔹 Interview questions
* 🔹 Real-world use cases
* 🔹 LINQ + Extension methods
* 🔹 Practice programs

# 📘 Difference Between **String** and **StringBuilder** in C#

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Ye topic kyun important hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, ye **interview ka bahut common question** hai, especially **entry-level C# developers** ke liye.
Isse **performance** aur **memory management** samajh aata hai.

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: String kya hoti hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

**String ek immutable object hoti hai**.

👉 **Immutable** ka matlab:

> Ek baar string create ho gayi, uski value change nahi hoti.

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Par hum to string ko modify karte hain na?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Dekhne me lagta hai modify ho rahi hai,
lekin **actually new string create hoti hai**.

### Example:

```csharp
string s = "Hello";
s = s + " World";
```

👉 Memory me kya hota hai:

* `"Hello"` → ek object
* `"Hello World"` → **new object**
* Old object delete nahi hota (GC handle karega)

✔ **Multiple copies create hoti hain**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Har modification pe kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Har modification pe nayi copy**

Example:

```csharp
s = "Hello";
s = s + " World";
s = s + " XYZ";
```

📌 Heap memory me:

* "Hello"
* "Hello World"
* "Hello World XYZ"

❌ Performance slow
❌ Memory waste

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: String kab use karni chahiye?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

✔ Jab **static value** ho
✔ Jab **few modifications** ho

Example:

```csharp
string country = "India";
```

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: StringBuilder kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

**StringBuilder ek mutable class hai**.

👉 **Mutable** ka matlab:

> Same object ke andar value modify hoti hai
> Nayi copy create nahi hoti

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: StringBuilder kaise create karte hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

```csharp
StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder("Hello");
```

✔ Internally **16 characters capacity** se start hota hai
✔ Remaining space future changes ke liye hota hai

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: StringBuilder me value kaise add karte hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

```csharp
sb.Append(" World");
sb.Append(" XYZ");
```

👉 Same object modify hota hai
👉 **No extra copies**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Capacity ka concept samjhao

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

* Default capacity = **16**
* Agar full ho jaye → **double ho jati hai**

📌 Example:
16 → 32 → 64 → 128 …

👉 Automatic resizing hota hai

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Agar hume pehle se size pata ho?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Best practice hai **capacity pehle se dena**.

```csharp
StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(10000);
```

✔ Kam resizing
✔ Better performance

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: String vs StringBuilder performance test dikhao

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

### String test:

```csharp
string s = "";
for(int i=1;i<=100000;i++)
{
    s = s + i;
}
```

### StringBuilder test:

```csharp
StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
for(int i=1;i<=100000;i++)
{
    sb.Append(i);
}
```

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Time measure kaise kiya?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

**Stopwatch class** se
(`System.Diagnostics` namespace)

```csharp
Stopwatch sw = new Stopwatch();
sw.Start();
// code
sw.Stop();
Console.WriteLine(sw.ElapsedMilliseconds);
```

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Result kya aaya?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

| Type              | Time     |
| ----------------- | -------- |
| **String**        | ~3386 ms |
| **StringBuilder** | ~27 ms   |

🔥 **StringBuilder 100x faster**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Dono ka main difference kya hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

| Feature     | **String**          | **StringBuilder**      |
| ----------- | ------------------- | ---------------------- |
| Mutability  | ❌ Immutable         | ✅ Mutable              |
| Memory      | High usage          | Low usage              |
| Performance | Slow (many changes) | Fast                   |
| Copies      | Multiple            | Single                 |
| Namespace   | `System`            | `System.Text`          |
| Best For    | Static text         | Frequent modifications |

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Final rule kya yaad rakhein?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

✔ **Few changes → String**
✔ **Many changes → StringBuilder**

---

## 🧠 Interview One-Line Answer

> **String is immutable and creates new objects on modification, whereas StringBuilder is mutable and modifies the same object, making it more efficient for frequent string manipulations.**

---

## ✅ Final Recommendation

👉 **Loops / large concatenation / performance-critical code**
➡️ **Always use StringBuilder**

* 🔹 Interview Q&A
* 🔹 Real-time use cases
* 🔹 String vs StringBuffer vs StringBuilder
* 🔹 MCQs

# 📘 Exceptions & Exception Handling in C#.NET (Part-1)

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Exception kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, normally log bolte hain **“exception is a runtime error”**,
lekin **ye 100% correct nahi hai** ❌

👉 **Exception runtime error nahi hota**,
👉 **Exception ek class hoti hai** jo **runtime error aane par program ko abnormally terminate karti hai**.

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Errors ke types kaunse hote hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

C# me mainly **2 types ke errors** hote hain:

1️⃣ **Compile Time Errors**
2️⃣ **Runtime Errors**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Compile Time Error kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Jo error **syntax mistakes** ki wajah se aata hai use **compile time error** kehte hain.

### Examples:

* Semicolon (`;`) bhool gaye
* Braces `{ }` close nahi ki
* String double quotes me nahi likhi

```csharp
Console.WriteLine("Hello")
```

❌ Semicolon missing → Compile time error

👉 **Ye dangerous nahi hota**,
kyunki **program run hi nahi hota jab tak error fix na ho**.

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Runtime Error kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Jo error **program execute hote time** aata hai use **runtime error** kehte hain.

👉 Compiler isse **pehle detect nahi karta**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Runtime errors kyun aate hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Runtime error aane ke **multiple reasons** ho sakte hain:

### 🔹 1️⃣ Wrong Logic Implementation

Example: Array out of bound

```csharp
int[] arr = {10,20,30,40,50};

for(int i=0; i<=arr.Length; i++)
{
    Console.WriteLine(arr[i]);
}
```

❌ `arr.Length = 5`
❌ Loop 0–5 (6 times) chalega
➡️ **IndexOutOfRangeException**

---

### 🔹 2️⃣ Wrong Input Supplied

Example:

```csharp
int x = int.Parse(Console.ReadLine());
```

User ne integer ke jagah **string / char** diya
➡️ **FormatException**

---

### 🔹 3️⃣ Missing Required Resources

Examples:

* File delete ho gayi
* Database server down
* Network issue

➡️ Runtime error

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Runtime errors dangerous kyun hote hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Kyuki **runtime error aate hi program abnormally terminate ho jata hai** ❌
Aur **baaki ka code execute hi nahi hota**.

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Real-life example do

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

💰 **Bank money transfer case**

Steps:
1️⃣ Source account se paisa deduct
2️⃣ Destination account me paisa add

Agar:

* Step-1 success
* Step-2 se pehle runtime error

👉 Paisa **kat gaya**, add **nahi hua**
👉 Customer problem
👉 **Serious issue**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Abnormal termination kaun karta hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Exceptions responsible hoti hain**

⚠️ **Runtime error ≠ Exception**

✔ Runtime error hota hai
✔ **Exception class activate hoti hai**
✔ Program ko **abnormally terminate** karti hai

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Exception kya hoti hai technically?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Exception ek class hoti hai**

Jab runtime error hota hai:

* CLR exception object create karta hai
* Ye object program ko terminate karta hai
* Error message show karta hai

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Kya sirf ek exception class hoti hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

❌ Nahi sir
👉 **Har type ke error ke liye alag exception class hoti hai**

### Examples:

* **IndexOutOfRangeException**
* **DivideByZeroException**
* **OverflowException**
* **FormatException**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Divide by zero ka case samjhao

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

```csharp
int a = 10;
int b = 0;
int c = a / b;
```

❌ Divide by zero allowed nahi
➡️ **DivideByZeroException**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Exception hierarchy explain karo

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

### 🔹 Parent class:

### 👉 **Exception**

Isme hota hai:

* **Abnormal termination logic**
* **Message property** (read-only, virtual)

---

### 🔹 Child classes:

1️⃣ **ApplicationException**
2️⃣ **SystemException**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: SystemException kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Fatal errors**
👉 CLR automatically throw karta hai

### Examples:

* DivideByZeroException
* IndexOutOfRangeException
* OverflowException
* FormatException

⚠️ Universal rules violate hone par aata hai

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: ApplicationException kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Non-fatal**
👉 **Programmer khud throw karta hai**

### Example:

* Application rule:
  ❌ “Odd number se divide nahi karna”

Ye rule:

* CLR ka nahi
* **Application specific**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Message property ka role kya hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

* `Exception` class me **Message property hoti hai**
* Ye **virtual** hoti hai
* Child classes **override** karti hain
* Har exception apna **custom error message** show karti hai

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Final conclusion bolo

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

✔ **Exception runtime error nahi hota**
✔ **Exception ek class hoti hai**
✔ **Runtime error aane par exception program terminate karti hai**
✔ **Different errors → different exception classes**

---

## 🧠 Interview One-Line Answer

> **Exception is a class that handles abnormal termination of a program when a runtime error occurs.**

---

## 🔜 Next Part me kya aayega?

👉 **Exception Handling**

* try
* catch
* finally
* multiple catch
* custom exceptions

Agar chaho to main **Part-2 ke notes bhi isi style me** bana deta hoon 👍

Main **Exception class ki hierarchy** ko **simple + clear + Hinglish** me samjha raha hoon, jaise exam / interview me poocha jata hai.

# 🧠 Exception Class Hierarchy in C# (.NET)

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Exception hierarchy kya hoti hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Exception hierarchy** matlab
**kaunsa exception kis class se inherit hota hai** (parent–child relationship).

---

## 🥇 ROOT / PARENT CLASS

### ✅ **System.Exception**

👉 **Sabse top / base / parent class**

* Ye **saari exceptions ka parent** hoti hai
* Isme hota hai:

  * Abnormal termination ka logic
  * **Message** property (error message)

```csharp
System.Exception
```

---

## 🥈 FIRST LEVEL CHILD CLASSES (2 main)

### 🔹 1️⃣ **System.SystemException**

### 🔹 2️⃣ **System.ApplicationException**

```text
Exception
 ├── SystemException
 └── ApplicationException
```

---

## 🥉 SystemException (IMPORTANT)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: SystemException kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **SystemException** wo exceptions hote hain jo
**CLR automatically throw karta hai**

✔ Ye **fatal errors** hote hain
✔ Universal rules break hone par aate hain

### Common child classes of **SystemException** 👇

```text
SystemException
 ├── ArithmeticException
 │    ├── DivideByZeroException
 │    └── OverflowException
 │
 ├── IndexOutOfRangeException
 ├── NullReferenceException
 ├── FormatException
 ├── InvalidCastException
 ├── OutOfMemoryException
 └── StackOverflowException
```

### Examples:

* Divide by zero → **DivideByZeroException**
* Array index out of bound → **IndexOutOfRangeException**
* Wrong type conversion → **FormatException**
* Null object access → **NullReferenceException**

---

## 🥉 ApplicationException

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: ApplicationException kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **ApplicationException** wo exceptions hote hain jo
**programmer khud define / throw karta hai**

✔ Ye **application-specific rules** ke liye hote hain
✔ CLR automatically throw nahi karta

### Example:

```text
Rule: Odd number se divide karna allowed nahi
```

Ye rule:

* CLR ka nahi
* Sirf **tumhari application ka rule**

👉 Iske liye **ApplicationException ya custom exception** use karte hain

---

## 🧾 COMPLETE HIERARCHY (EXAM READY)

```text
System.Object
   |
   └── System.Exception   ← ROOT / PARENT
         |
         ├── SystemException   ← CLR exceptions
         |      |
         |      ├── ArithmeticException
         |      |      ├── DivideByZeroException
         |      |      └── OverflowException
         |      |
         |      ├── IndexOutOfRangeException
         |      ├── NullReferenceException
         |      ├── FormatException
         |      └── InvalidCastException
         |
         └── ApplicationException   ← Programmer-defined
```

---

## 🧠 Important Points (Yaad rakhne ke liye)

✔ **Exception** → Parent class
✔ **SystemException** → CLR errors
✔ **ApplicationException** → Programmer errors
✔ **Most real errors = SystemException**
✔ **Message property sab exceptions me hoti hai**

---

## 🎯 Interview One-Line Answer

> **System.Exception is the parent class. It has two main child classes: SystemException and ApplicationException. SystemException has many built-in child exceptions like DivideByZeroException, IndexOutOfRangeException, etc.**

---

* ✔ Multiple catch
* ✔ Custom exception
* ✔ Real-time examples

# 📘 Exception Handling in C# – Part 2 (Q&A Notes)

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Runtime exception kaise occur hoti hai program me?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 Jab program **execute** ho raha hota hai aur
koi **illegal / invalid operation** hoti hai, tab **runtime exception** aati hai.

Examples:

* Divide by zero
* Wrong input (string instead of number)
* Value overflow

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Exception aane par internally kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

1. Program **CLR (Common Language Runtime)** ke under run hota hai
2. CLR line-by-line code ko monitor karta hai
3. Jaise hi runtime error milta hai:

   * CLR **matching Exception class** choose karta hai
   * Us class ka **object create** karta hai
   * Us object ko **throw** karta hai

👉 Ye object program ko **abnormally terminate** karta hai

---

## 🧠 Example: Divide by Zero

```csharp
int x = 100;
int y = 0;
int z = x / y;   // Exception yaha aayegi
Console.WriteLine("End of program");
```

❌ Output:

```text
Unhandled Exception: System.DivideByZeroException
```

👉 `"End of program"` print nahi hota
👉 **Abnormal termination**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Exception handling kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Exception handling** ka matlab hai
**runtime error aane par program ko crash hone se bachana**

---

## 🎯 Exception Handling ke 3 Major Advantages

### ✅ 1️⃣ Abnormal termination ruk jata hai

👉 Error ke baad bhi remaining code execute hota hai

### ✅ 2️⃣ User-friendly error message dikha sakte hain

👉 Technical message ki jagah simple message

### ✅ 3️⃣ Corrective actions perform kar sakte hain

👉 Example: **Transaction rollback**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Exception handle kaise karte hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **try–catch blocks** ka use karke

---

## 🧱 try–catch ka basic structure

```csharp
try
{
    // Risky code (jahan exception aa sakti hai)
}
catch (ExceptionType ex)
{
    // Error handling code
}
```

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: try block me kya likhna chahiye?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

✔ Wo statements:

* Jo **runtime error cause** kar sakti hain
* Jo **error aane par execute nahi honi chahiye**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: catch block me kya likhna chahiye?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

✔ Wo code:

* Jo **sirf exception aane par** execute hona chahiye
* Error message
* Corrective actions

---

## 🧪 Complete Example with try–catch

```csharp
try
{
    Console.WriteLine("Enter first number:");
    int x = int.Parse(Console.ReadLine());

    Console.WriteLine("Enter second number:");
    int y = int.Parse(Console.ReadLine());

    int z = x / y;
    Console.WriteLine("Result is: " + z);
}
catch (DivideByZeroException ex)
{
    Console.WriteLine(ex.Message);
}

Console.WriteLine("End of program");
```

### Output (100 / 0):

```text
Attempted to divide by zero.
End of program
```

✔ Program crash nahi hua
✔ End of program print hua

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Multiple exceptions kaise handle kare?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Multiple catch blocks** use karke

---

## 🧱 Multiple catch example

```csharp
try
{
    int x = int.Parse(Console.ReadLine());
    int y = int.Parse(Console.ReadLine());
    int z = x / y;
}
catch (DivideByZeroException)
{
    Console.WriteLine("Zero se divide mat karo");
}
catch (FormatException)
{
    Console.WriteLine("Input numeric hona chahiye");
}
```

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Agar koi unknown exception aa jaye to?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Generic catch (Exception)** use karte hain

```csharp
catch (Exception ex)
{
    Console.WriteLine(ex.Message);
}
```

✔ Ye **sab exceptions catch** kar leta hai
✔ But hamesha **last me** likhna chahiye

---

## ⚠️ Important Rule (Interview Favorite)

❌ Generic catch ko upar mat rakho
✔ Hamesha **specific → generic** order follow karo

```csharp
catch (DivideByZeroException) { }
catch (FormatException) { }
catch (Exception) { }   // LAST
```

---

## 🧠 Real-life Analogy (Cricket example)

* CLR = Bowler
* Exception object = Cricket ball
* catch block = Fielder

👉 Agar fielder ball catch kar leta hai → glass (program) nahi toot ta

---

## 📝 Summary (One Look Revision)

✔ Runtime error → CLR exception object throw karta hai
✔ try block → risky code
✔ catch block → error handling
✔ Multiple catch → different actions
✔ catch(Exception) → sabse last me

---

## 🎯 Interview One-liner

> **Exception handling in C# is done using try–catch blocks to prevent abnormal termination, display user-friendly messages, and perform corrective actions.**

---

* 🔜 `finally block`
* 🔜 `throw vs throws`
* 🔜 `Custom Exception`
* 🔜 `Real-time banking example`

# 📘 Exception Handling in C# – Part 3 (Q&A Notes)

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Part-3 me hum kya seekhne wale hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 Is part me hum samjhenge:

* **try–catch execution flow**
* **finally block kya hota hai**
* **finally kab aur kyun use karte hain**
* **try–catch–finally combinations**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: try–catch block me execution kaise hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Execution **2 scenarios** me hota hai:

---

## ✅ Scenario 1: **try block me koi exception nahi aayi**

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

* try ke **saare statements successfully execute** ho jaate hain
* **koi catch block execute nahi hota**
* Control **directly catch ke baad wali line** par jump karta hai

### 🔹 Flow:

```text
try → success → skip all catch → next statement
```

---

## ❌ Scenario 2: **try block me exception aa gayi**

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

* Jis line par exception aayi, wahi se execution ruk jata hai
* Control **catch blocks** me jump karta hai
* Matching catch mil gaya → exception handle
* Catch ke baad wali statement execute hoti hai

### 🔹 Flow:

```text
try → exception → matching catch → next statement
```

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Agar matching catch block na mile to?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Abnormal termination** ho jaati hai
👉 Program wahi ruk jata hai

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: finally block kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **finally block** wo block hota hai jo:

✔ **Exception aaye ya na aaye**
✔ **Har haal me execute hota hai**

Isliye ise bolte hain:

### ⭐ **Mandatory execution block**

---

## 🧱 Structure: try–catch–finally

```csharp
try
{
    // Risky code
}
catch (Exception ex)
{
    // Error handling
}
finally
{
    // Mandatory code
}
```

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: finally block ki zarurat kyun padi?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Kyunki kuch code aisa hota hai jo:

* Exception aaye ya na aaye
* **Har condition me execute hona chahiye**

---

## 🧠 Real-life Example: File Handling

### Steps:

1️⃣ File open
2️⃣ File me write
3️⃣ File close

---

### ❓ Problem:

* Write ke time exception aa sakti hai
* Agar exception aayi → file close nahi hogi

---

### ✅ Solution:

👉 **File close code finally block me likho**

---

## 🧪 finally Block Example

```csharp
try
{
    int x = int.Parse(Console.ReadLine());
    int y = int.Parse(Console.ReadLine());
    int z = x / y;
    Console.WriteLine(z);
}
catch (Exception ex)
{
    Console.WriteLine(ex.Message);
}
finally
{
    Console.WriteLine("Finally block executed");
}

Console.WriteLine("End of program");
```

---

## 🔍 Output Analysis

### Case 1: No Exception (100 / 5)

```text
20
Finally block executed
End of program
```

---

### Case 2: Exception (100 / 0)

```text
Attempted to divide by zero.
Finally block executed
End of program
```

👉 finally **dono case me execute hua**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Agar return statement aa jaye to?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Return ek jump statement hai**
Par **finally se escape nahi kar sakta**

---

## 🧪 Example: return + finally

```csharp
try
{
    if (y == 1)
        return;
}
finally
{
    Console.WriteLine("Finally block executed");
}
```

### 🔹 Output:

```text
Finally block executed
```

✔ return hone se pehle bhi finally execute hota hai

---

## ⭐ Golden Rule (Interview Favorite)

> **If control enters try, finally will execute compulsory.**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: try–catch–finally ke combinations kaunse hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

---

## 1️⃣ try–catch

✔ Exception handle hoti hai
✔ Abnormal termination nahi hota
❌ Mandatory execution ka option nahi

---

## 2️⃣ try–catch–finally ✅ (MOST USED)

✔ Exception handle hoti hai
✔ finally hamesha execute hota hai
✔ Best & safest option

---

## 3️⃣ try–finally

✔ finally execute hota hai
❌ Exception handle nahi hoti
❌ Abnormal termination hota hai

👉 Use jab:

* Exception handle nahi karni
* Resource release **mandatory** ho

---

## 🧠 try–finally Example (No catch)

```csharp
try
{
    int x = 10 / 0;
}
finally
{
    Console.WriteLine("Finally executed");
}
```

### Output:

```text
Finally executed
Unhandled Exception
```

✔ finally execute hua
❌ program crash hua

---

## 📌 finally block ka real use

✔ File close
✔ Database connection close
✔ Network connection close
✔ Resource release

---

## 📝 Summary (Quick Revision)

✔ finally = mandatory execution
✔ Exception aaye ya na aaye → finally chalega
✔ return bhi finally ko skip nahi kar sakta
✔ Best practice → **try–catch–finally**

---

## 🎯 Interview One-Liner

> **The finally block in C# is used to execute mandatory code such as resource cleanup, and it executes whether an exception occurs or not, even with return statements.**

---

* 🔜 `Custom Exceptions`
* 🔜 `Real-time banking transaction example`

# 📘 Exception Handling in C# – Part 4

## (Application Exceptions & Custom Exceptions)

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Part-4 me hum kya seekhne wale hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 Is part me hum seekhenge:

* **System Exception vs Application Exception**
* **Application Exception kya hota hai**
* **Programmer khud exception kaise throw karta hai**
* **throw keyword**
* **Custom Exception class kaise banate hain**
* **User-friendly error messages kaise dete hain**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Exceptions ko kitni categories me divide kiya gaya hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Exceptions **2 categories** me divide hoti hain:

1️⃣ **System Exceptions**
2️⃣ **Application Exceptions**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: System Exception kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **System Exception** wo exception hoti hai:

* Jo **CLR (Common Language Runtime)** khud throw karta hai
* **Predefined rules** ke basis par

### 🔹 Examples:

* **DivideByZeroException**
* **FormatException**
* **OverflowException**
* **NullReferenceException**

📌 Example:

```csharp
int x = 10 / 0;   // CLR throw karega DivideByZeroException
```

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Application Exception kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Application Exception** wo exception hoti hai:

* Jo **CLR nahi**,
* **Programmer / Developer khud throw karta hai**
* **Application-specific rules** ke liye

📌 Matlab:

> Rule universal nahi hai, sirf hamari application ke liye hai.

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: CLR application exception kyun nahi throw karta?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Kyunki:

* CLR sirf **universal rules** jaanta hai
* Jaise: divide by zero ❌
* Par **odd number se divide karna allowed hai** (CLR ke liye)

👉 Agar mujhe odd number allow nahi karna → **mujhe khud exception throw karni padegi**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Application Exception kaise throw karte hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **throw keyword** ka use karke

---

## 🧪 Example: Odd Number Divisor Not Allowed

```csharp
if (y % 2 != 0)
{
    throw new ApplicationException();
}
```

### Output:

```text
System.ApplicationException: Error in the application
```

⚠️ Message user-friendly nahi hai

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: User-friendly error message kaise dete hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **ApplicationException constructor me message pass karke**

---

## 🧪 Example: Custom Error Message

```csharp
if (y % 2 != 0)
{
    throw new ApplicationException("Divisor value cannot be odd number");
}
```

### Output:

```text
System.ApplicationException: Divisor value cannot be odd number
```

✔ Ab message clear hai

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: throw ex aur throw new Exception me kya difference?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

### 🔹 Method-1 (Named object)

```csharp
ApplicationException ex =
    new ApplicationException("Error");
throw ex;
```

### 🔹 Method-2 (Unnamed object – BEST)

```csharp
throw new ApplicationException("Error");
```

📌 Jab members access nahi karne → **direct throw new** best

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: ApplicationException ka naam descriptive nahi lagta, kya solution hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Custom Exception class banana**

---

## 🏗️ Custom Exception Banane ke Steps

### ✅ Step-1: Exception class create karo

```csharp
public class DivideByOddNumberException
    : ApplicationException
{
    public override string Message
    {
        get
        {
            return "Attempted to divide by odd number";
        }
    }
}
```

📌 Points:

* **ApplicationException se inherit**
* **Message property override**
* **Message read-only hoti hai**

---

### ✅ Step-2: Custom Exception throw karo

```csharp
if (y % 2 != 0)
{
    throw new DivideByOddNumberException();
}
```

---

## 🧪 Complete Example

```csharp
int x = int.Parse(Console.ReadLine());
int y = int.Parse(Console.ReadLine());

if (y % 2 != 0)
{
    throw new DivideByOddNumberException();
}

Console.WriteLine(x / y);
```

---

## 📤 Output Examples

### Case 1: `100 / 10`

```text
10
```

### Case 2: `100 / 0`

```text
System.DivideByZeroException:
Attempted to divide by zero
```

### Case 3: `100 / 5`

```text
DivideByOddNumberException:
Attempted to divide by odd number
```

✔ **Exception name + message dono descriptive**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Namespace ka role kya hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

* **System** → CLR exceptions
* **Custom namespace** → Developer exceptions

```csharp
namespace MyApp.Exceptions
{
    public class DivideByOddNumberException : ApplicationException
    {
        ...
    }
}
```

Output me namespace bhi dikhega

---

## 📌 Summary (Quick Revision)

✔ **System Exception** → CLR throw karta hai
✔ **Application Exception** → Programmer throw karta hai
✔ **throw keyword** use hota hai
✔ **ApplicationException** base class hoti hai
✔ **Custom Exception** se:

* Name descriptive
* Message user-friendly
* Code readable

---

## 🎯 Interview One-Liners

> **Application exceptions are thrown by developers to handle application-specific business rules.**

> **Custom exceptions improve code readability and provide meaningful error messages.**

---

* 🔜 `throw vs throw ex`
* 🔜 `Exception chaining`
* 🔜 `Real-time banking / project scenarios`
* 🔜 `Exception handling interview Q&A`

# 📘 Multithreading in C# – Part 1

## (Concepts, Need & Single vs Multi Thread)

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Multithreading samajhne se pehle hume kya samajhna chahiye?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 Hume pehle **Multitasking** samajhna chahiye.

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Multitasking kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Multitasking** ka matlab:

* Ek hi time par **multiple applications** chalna
* Jaise:

  * Notepad
  * Chrome
  * Visual Studio
  * File Explorer

✔ Windows OS ek **Multitasking Operating System** hai

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: OS itne saare applications kaise run karta hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 OS **Process** ka use karta hai

---

## 🧠 **Process kya hota hai?**

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Process** ek OS component hai jo:

* Ek application ko execute karta hai
* Har running application ke liye **alag process**

📌 Task Manager → Processes tab me dikhta hai

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Background me chalne wale programs kaunse hote hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 Unhe bolte hain **Background Processes / Windows Services**

* Database services
* Web servers
* System services

📌 Control Panel → Administrative Tools → Services

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Process ke andar code kaun execute karta hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Thread**

---

## 🧵 **Thread kya hota hai?**

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Thread** ek unit hai jo:

* Application ke **code/logic ko execute** karta hai
* Process ke andar hota hai

📌 Simple words me:

> **Thread = Code execute karne wala worker**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: By default har application me kitne threads hote hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Sirf 1 thread**

* Ise bolte hain **Main Thread**
* Isliye by default har application **Single-Threaded** hoti hai

---

## 🧪 Example: Default Main Thread

```csharp
using System;
using System.Threading;

class Demo
{
    static void Main()
    {
        Thread t = Thread.CurrentThread;
        t.Name = "Main Thread";

        Console.WriteLine("Current Thread: " + t.Name);
    }
}
```

### 📝 Notes:

* **Thread.CurrentThread** → current executing thread
* Default thread ka naam nahi hota
* Hum manually name set kar sakte hain

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Single-Threaded application kaise kaam karti hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 Single thread:

* Saara code **one by one (sequential)** execute karta hai
* Ek method complete hone ke baad hi next method start hota hai

---

## 🧪 Example: Single Thread Execution

```csharp
static void Test1()
{
    for (int i = 1; i <= 100; i++)
        Console.WriteLine("Test1: " + i);
}

static void Test2()
{
    for (int i = 1; i <= 100; i++)
        Console.WriteLine("Test2: " + i);
}

static void Test3()
{
    for (int i = 1; i <= 100; i++)
        Console.WriteLine("Test3: " + i);
}

static void Main()
{
    Test1();
    Test2();
    Test3();
}
```

### 📤 Output Order:

```
Test1 complete
Test2 complete
Test3 complete
```

✔ One after another

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Single-Threaded model ka drawback kya hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Blocking / Delay problem**

---

## ❌ Problem Scenario (Real-Time)

* Test2 method:

  * Database call
  * Server busy
  * 10–15 seconds delay

👉 Is delay ke dauran:

* Test3 execute nahi hoga
* CPU idle rahega
* Performance waste hogi

---

## 🧪 Simulation using `Thread.Sleep()`

```csharp
static void Test2()
{
    for (int i = 1; i <= 100; i++)
    {
        if (i == 50)
        {
            Console.WriteLine("Main thread sleeping...");
            Thread.Sleep(5000); // 5 seconds
            Console.WriteLine("Main thread woke up");
        }
        Console.WriteLine("Test2: " + i);
    }
}
```

### 🧠 Observation:

* Program **5 seconds ruk jaata hai**
* Test3 tab tak start nahi hota

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Is problem ka solution kya hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Multithreading**

---

## 🔁 **Multithreading kya hota hai?**

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Multithreading** ka matlab:

* Ek process ke andar **multiple threads**
* Har thread **alag-alag kaam** karta hai

📌 Example:

* Test1 → Thread1
* Test2 → Thread2
* Test3 → Thread3

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Multithreading kaise kaam karti hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Time Sharing** ke basis par

* OS har thread ko **thoda-thoda CPU time** deta hai
* Sab threads **almost simultaneously** run karte hain

---

## ✅ Multithreading ke Advantages

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

✔ CPU ka **maximum utilization**
✔ Ek thread delay ho → baaki threads kaam karte rahenge
✔ Application **responsive** hoti hai
✔ Performance better hoti hai

---

## 📌 Single Thread vs Multi Thread

| Single Thread      | Multi Thread        |
| ------------------ | ------------------- |
| One task at a time | Multiple tasks      |
| Blocking issue     | No blocking         |
| CPU idle time      | CPU fully used      |
| Slow               | Faster & responsive |

---

## 🎯 Interview One-Liners

👉 **Thread is the smallest unit of execution inside a process.**

👉 **By default, every C# application is single-threaded.**

👉 **Multithreading improves CPU utilization using time-sharing.**

---

## 🔜 Next Part Me Kya Aayega?

* Thread creation
* `ThreadStart`
* Multiple threads with methods
* Real coding examples

Agar chaho to bolna:
**“Multithreading Part-2 notes chahiye”** 👍

# 📘 Multithreading in C# – Part 2

## (Creating Threads & Executing Methods using Threads)

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Pichhle video me humne kya seekha tha?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 Humne seekha tha ki:

* By default har C# program **single-threaded** hota hai
* **Main Thread** saare methods ko **one by one** execute karta hai
* Agar ek method slow ho jaaye, to baaki methods **wait** karte rehte hain

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Single-threaded model ka main problem kya hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Time waste aur CPU under-utilization**

* Ek method delay hui → baaki methods ruk jaate hain
* CPU idle rehta hai
* Performance slow ho jaati hai

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Is problem ka solution kya hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Multithreading**

---

## 🧠 **Multithreading ka basic idea kya hai?**

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 Ek process ke andar **multiple threads** bana ke:

* Har method ko **alag thread** se execute karna
* OS har thread ko **time share** karta hai
* Sab methods **simultaneously (time-sharing)** execute hote hain

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Multithreading me methods kaise execute hote hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 Example:

* `Test1()` → Thread-1
* `Test2()` → Thread-2
* `Test3()` → Thread-3

OS in teenon ko **equal priority** deta hai

---

## ⏱ **Time Sharing kya hota hai?**

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 OS:

* Thread-1 ko thoda CPU time deta hai
* Phir Thread-2
* Phir Thread-3
* Phir wapas Thread-1

📌 Isliye output **har run me different** hota hai

---

## 🧪 Step-1: Teen methods define karna

```csharp
using System;
using System.Threading;

class ThreadTest
{
    static void Test1()
    {
        for (int i = 1; i <= 100; i++)
            Console.WriteLine("Test1 : " + i);

        Console.WriteLine("Thread 1 exiting");
    }

    static void Test2()
    {
        for (int i = 1; i <= 100; i++)
        {
            if (i == 50)
            {
                Console.WriteLine("Thread 2 going to sleep");
                Thread.Sleep(5000);
                Console.WriteLine("Thread 2 woke up");
            }
            Console.WriteLine("Test2 : " + i);
        }

        Console.WriteLine("Thread 2 exiting");
    }

    static void Test3()
    {
        for (int i = 1; i <= 100; i++)
            Console.WriteLine("Test3 : " + i);

        Console.WriteLine("Thread 3 exiting");
    }
```

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Ab threads kaise create karenge?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Thread class** ka use karke

---

## 🧪 Step-2: Threads create karna

```csharp
static void Main()
{
    Thread t1 = new Thread(Test1);
    Thread t2 = new Thread(Test2);
    Thread t3 = new Thread(Test3);
```

### 📝 Notes:

* `new Thread(MethodName)`
* Method **automatically execute** hoga jab thread start hoga

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Threads create karne ke baad kya hoga?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 Kuch bhi execute nahi hoga 😄
👉 Hume **threads start** karni padengi

---

## 🧪 Step-3: Threads start karna

```csharp
    t1.Start();
    t2.Start();
    t3.Start();

    Console.WriteLine("Main thread exiting");
}
```

---

## 🔍 Program me total kitne threads hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **4 Threads**

| Thread      | Kaam                   |
| ----------- | ---------------------- |
| Main Thread | Threads create & start |
| Thread-1    | Test1                  |
| Thread-2    | Test2                  |
| Thread-3    | Test3                  |

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Output same order me aayega kya?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

❌ **No**

👉 Har run me output **different** hoga
👉 Kyunki scheduling **Operating System** control karta hai

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Agar ek thread sleep kare to baaki threads ka kya hoga?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 Baaki threads **continue** karte rahenge

* `Thread.Sleep()` sirf **current thread** ko affect karta hai
* CPU waste nahi hota

---

## 🧠 Important Point: `Thread.Sleep()`

```csharp
Thread.Sleep(5000);
```

* **Static method**
* Instance se call nahi hota (`t2.Sleep()` ❌)
* Jo thread method execute kar raha hota hai **wahi sleep** karta hai

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Thread-2 sleep me hai, Thread-1 & Thread-3 kya karte hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 Wo **normally execute** hote rehte hain

✔ CPU ka **maximum utilization** hota hai

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Main thread kab exit hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 Jab:

* Threads create ho jaate hain
* Threads start ho jaate hain

📌 Main thread ka kaam yahin khatam

---

## 🧠 Observation (Execution Flow)

1️⃣ Main thread start
2️⃣ Threads create
3️⃣ Threads start
4️⃣ Main thread exit
5️⃣ Child threads apna kaam complete karke exit

---

## ✅ Multithreading ke Fayde (Recap)

✔ CPU ka best use
✔ Blocking nahi hoti
✔ Faster execution
✔ Real-time applications ke liye best

---

## 🧪 Real-Time Example

| Scenario      | Single Thread | Multi Thread    |
| ------------- | ------------- | --------------- |
| DB slow       | App stuck     | App responsive  |
| Network delay | Freeze        | Other tasks run |
| Heavy task    | Slow          | Fast            |

---

## 🎯 Interview One-Liners

👉 **Multithreading allows multiple methods to execute using multiple threads inside a single process.**

👉 **Thread.Sleep pauses only the current executing thread.**

👉 **Thread scheduling is controlled by the Operating System.**

👉 **Output order in multithreading is unpredictable.**

---

## 🔜 Next Part (Part-3 me kya aayega?)

* Thread constructor variations
* Parameterized threads
* Thread priorities
* Thread lifecycle

# 📘 Multithreading in C# – Part 3

## (Thread Class Constructors & Delegates)

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Aaj ka topic kya hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 Aaj hum **Thread class ke constructors** aur
👉 **ThreadStart** aur **ParameterizedThreadStart delegates** ke baare me seekhenge.

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Sabse pehle simple method likhte hain, kya hoga?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 Ek simple method jo numbers print karega.

```csharp
static void Test()
{
    for (int i = 1; i <= 100; i++)
        Console.WriteLine("Test : " + i);
}
```

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Is method ko normally kaise call karte hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Main thread** se directly call kar dete hain.

```csharp
static void Main()
{
    Test();
}
```

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Lekin hum is method ko **child thread** se call karna chahte hain. Kaise?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Thread class** ka use karke.

---

## 🔹 Step-1: Required namespace import karo

```csharp
using System.Threading;
```

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Thread class ke constructors kitne hote hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **4 constructors** hote hain, lekin main important ye 2 hain:

1️⃣ `Thread(ThreadStart start)`
2️⃣ `Thread(ParameterizedThreadStart start)`

---

## 🧠 Important Concept: **ThreadStart Delegate**

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: ThreadStart kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **ThreadStart ek delegate hai**

* **Delegate = type-safe function pointer**
* Jo method call karega uska **signature match** hona chahiye

### ✔ Signature:

```csharp
void MethodName();
```

* No parameter
* No return value

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Test() method ka signature match karta hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

✔ Yes

```csharp
static void Test()
```

* void return
* no parameter

---

## 🔹 Option-1: Direct method pass karna (Most common)

```csharp
Thread t1 = new Thread(Test);
t1.Start();
```

📌 **CLR internally ThreadStart delegate create karta hai**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: But constructor me method name directly kaise pass ho raha hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 Internally **CLR delegate create karta hai**

Equivalent code:

```csharp
ThreadStart obj = new ThreadStart(Test);
Thread t1 = new Thread(obj);
t1.Start();
```

---

## 🔹 Delegate Instantiation ka meaning

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Delegate instantiation kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **Method ko delegate ke saath bind karna**

---

## 🔹 Delegate banane ke different tareeke

### ✔ Way-1 (Normal)

```csharp
ThreadStart obj = new ThreadStart(Test);
```

### ✔ Way-2 (Short)

```csharp
ThreadStart obj = Test;
```

### ✔ Way-3 (Anonymous Method – C# 2.0)

```csharp
ThreadStart obj = delegate ()
{
    for (int i = 1; i <= 100; i++)
        Console.WriteLine(i);
};
```

### ✔ Way-4 (Lambda Expression – Modern way)

```csharp
ThreadStart obj = () =>
{
    for (int i = 1; i <= 100; i++)
        Console.WriteLine(i);
};
```

📌 **Sab same kaam karte hain**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Ab parameter wali method ka kya?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 Tab **ThreadStart kaam nahi karega**

---

## 🔹 ParameterizedThreadStart Delegate

### ✔ Signature:

```csharp
void Method(object obj);
```

---

## 🧪 Example: Parameter wali method

```csharp
static void Test(object max)
{
    int number = Convert.ToInt32(max);

    for (int i = 1; i <= number; i++)
        Console.WriteLine(i);
}
```

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Thread kaise create karoge?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 **ParameterizedThreadStart** use karke

```csharp
Thread t1 = new Thread(Test);
t1.Start(50);
```

📌 Output: `1 to 50`

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Start(50) ka matlab kya?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

👉 `50` object ke roop me method ko pass hota hai

---

## ⚠️ Important Warning: **Type Safety Problem**

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher: Agar galat type pass kar diya to?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

❌ Compile-time error nahi aayega
❌ Runtime error aayega

```csharp
t1.Start("hello"); // ❌ Runtime error
```

📌 Kyunki `object` kuch bhi accept karta hai

---

## 🧠 Summary Table

| Delegate                 | Parameter | Type Safety |
| ------------------------ | --------- | ----------- |
| ThreadStart              | ❌ No      | ✔ Safe      |
| ParameterizedThreadStart | ✔ Object  | ❌ Not safe  |

---

## 🎯 Interview Important Points

👉 **ThreadStart delegate is used for methods without parameters**
👉 **ParameterizedThreadStart allows passing parameters as object**
👉 **CLR implicitly creates delegate when method name is passed**
👉 **ParameterizedThreadStart is not type-safe**
👉 **Lambda expressions are modern and preferred**

---

## 🔚 Final One-Line Summary

👉 **Thread class constructors internally work using delegates, and methods are executed by binding them with ThreadStart or ParameterizedThreadStart delegates.**

---
