# 📘 Topic: **LINQ to SQL – Part 4 (CRUD: Insert, Update, Delete)**

---

## 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Ab tak LINQ to SQL me hum kya-kya kar chuke hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

Sir, ab tak humne seekha:

* **ORM (Object Relational Mapping)**
* **DataContext** ka use
* **Table → Class**
* **Row → Object**
* **GridView me data load karna**
* **TextBoxes me row-by-row data dikhana**

Aaj hum **CRUD operations** seekhne wale hain.

---

## 🔹 Concept 1: **CRUD kya hota hai?**

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

CRUD ka matlab kya hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

| CRUD           | Meaning |
| -------------- | ------- |
| **C – Create** | Insert  |
| **R – Read**   | Select  |
| **U – Update** | Update  |
| **D – Delete** | Delete  |

LINQ to SQL sirf **select** hi nahi, balki **insert, update, delete** bhi support karta hai.

---

## 🔹 Application Design (Overview)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Is example me forms ka structure kya hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

### 🧾 **Form-3**

* DataGridView (`DGView`)
* Buttons: **Insert, Update, Delete, Close**
* GridView → **Read-only**

### ✏️ **Form-4**

* 5 TextBoxes (EmpNo, Name, Job, Salary, Dept)
* Buttons: **Save, Clear, Close**
* Insert & Update dono ke liye use hota hai

---

## 🔹 Step 1: **Form-3 Load – Read Operation**

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Form load hote hi data kaise dikhate hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

```csharp
CompanyDBDataContext dc = new CompanyDBDataContext();
DGView.DataSource = dc.Employees;
DGView.ReadOnly = true;
```

📌 **Employees** ek property hai jo `Table<Employee>` return karti hai.

---

## 🔹 Step 2: **INSERT Operation (Create)**

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

LINQ to SQL me record insert kaise karte hain?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

### ✅ Steps:

1. **Employee class ka object banao**
2. **TextBox values assign karo**
3. **InsertOnSubmit() call karo**
4. **SubmitChanges() se commit karo**

### 🧪 Example (Form-4 → Save button):

```csharp
Employee obj = new Employee();

obj.EmpNo  = int.Parse(txtEmpNo.Text);
obj.Name   = txtName.Text;
obj.Job    = txtJob.Text;
obj.Salary = decimal.Parse(txtSalary.Text);
obj.DName  = txtDept.Text;

dc.Employees.InsertOnSubmit(obj);
dc.SubmitChanges();

MessageBox.Show("Record Inserted");
```

📌

* `InsertOnSubmit()` → **Pending insert**
* `SubmitChanges()` → **Commit to DB**

---

## 🔹 Clear Button Logic (Insert Form)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Clear button sab TextBoxes kaise clear karta hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

```csharp
foreach(Control ctrl in this.Controls)
{
    if(ctrl is TextBox tb)
        tb.Clear();
}
txtEmpNo.Focus();
```

---

## 🔹 Reload Data After Insert

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Insert ke baad GridView refresh kaise hota hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

* `Form4.ShowDialog()` ke baad
* Control wapas **Form-3** me aata hai
* Wahi pe **LoadData()** call karte hain

```csharp
void LoadData()
{
    DGView.DataSource = dc.Employees;
}
```

---

## 🔹 Step 3: **UPDATE Operation**

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Update ke time naya object banana chahiye?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

❌ Nahi sir

👉 **Existing record ka reference chahiye**

---

### 🔑 Important Rule:

> **Update = Existing object modify**

---

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Existing record ka reference kaise milta hai?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

`SingleOrDefault()` method se:

```csharp
Employee obj = dc.Employees
    .SingleOrDefault(e => e.EmpNo == int.Parse(txtEmpNo.Text));
```

---

### ✏️ Update Code (Save Button – Update Case):

```csharp
obj.Name   = txtName.Text;
obj.Job    = txtJob.Text;
obj.Salary = decimal.Parse(txtSalary.Text);
obj.DName  = txtDept.Text;

dc.SubmitChanges();
MessageBox.Show("Record Updated");
```

📌

* **EmpNo ReadOnly** hota hai
* Clear button **Disable**
* Save button text → **Update**

---

## 🔹 Insert vs Update – Condition Logic

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Insert aur Update code same button me kaise likhen?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

```csharp
if(txtEmpNo.ReadOnly == false)
{
    // INSERT logic
}
else
{
    // UPDATE logic
}
```

---

## 🔹 Step 4: **DELETE Operation**

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Delete ke liye alag form chahiye?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

❌ Nahi sir
Delete **direct Form-3** se hota hai.

---

### 🧪 Delete Steps:

1. Record select hona chahiye
2. Confirmation message
3. Record reference lo
4. `DeleteOnSubmit()`
5. `SubmitChanges()`

---

### 🧪 Delete Code:

```csharp
int eno = Convert.ToInt32(
    DGView.SelectedRows[0].Cells[0].Value);

Employee obj = dc.Employees
    .SingleOrDefault(e => e.EmpNo == eno);

dc.Employees.DeleteOnSubmit(obj);
dc.SubmitChanges();

LoadData();
```

---

### 🛑 Confirmation Message:

```csharp
if(MessageBox.Show(
   "Are you sure to delete?",
   "Confirm",
   MessageBoxButtons.YesNo,
   MessageBoxIcon.Question) == DialogResult.Yes)
{
   // Delete logic
}
```

---

## 🔹 Safety Checks (Very Important)

### 👨‍🏫 Teacher:

Agar record select nahi kiya to?

### 👨‍🎓 Student:

```csharp
if(DGView.SelectedRows.Count > 0)
{
   // Proceed
}
else
{
   MessageBox.Show("Please select a record");
}
```

---

## ⭐ Summary – CRUD Mapping

| Operation | LINQ Method                       |
| --------- | --------------------------------- |
| Insert    | `InsertOnSubmit()`                |
| Update    | Modify object + `SubmitChanges()` |
| Delete    | `DeleteOnSubmit()`                |
| Commit    | `SubmitChanges()`                 |

---

## 🧠 Interview One-Liner

> **“LINQ to SQL performs CRUD operations using object manipulation where records are treated as objects and changes are committed using SubmitChanges().”**
